## S2-P7 Line toggles (B's 「表示」)

Status: **dropped by its cut rule** (clause 1; results below). Started from `cd4aaaf6` (S2-P6's head, tree clean, frontend tree `c4e4131e`). Plan S2-P7; §5.6 (the fifth button never matches
/^週足/); B's prototype (the range tabs and 「表示」 share one row; the button reads 「表示 すべて」 or 「表示 n項目」, never n/m).

### The cut rule and its bases, stated before any run

- **Clause 1, the probe with every layer on.** The plan says "byte-identical to S2-P3's". The default probe's base is S2-P3's
  own file: `probe-a592864.json` 163d66cb…, which the S2-P6 head reproduces (`probe-cd4aaaf.json`, the same sha). The oracle
  probe's S2-P3 file (d36a6c5a…) is stale: S2-P5 changed ZZPG's readout text by its stated rename, so the oracle base is the head
  before this package, `probe-cd4aaaf-oracles.json` 57b75ca7…. Both are compared with `cmp`.
- **Clause 2, the periods row at 320.** Every button of the row, the fifth included, is at least 44 by 44px; the five share one
  top (one row); no button's text spills (`scrollWidth <= clientWidth`) and the row and the card do not overflow.
- **Clause 3, the card's height.** S2-P2b did not run (S2-P2 stopped), so the card keeps 508 / 476 / 492 (the exact pins of
  `brief-chart.spec.js`) and every card height the default probe records at 320 / 375 / 390 / 1440.

### Expected diff, stated before the run

The probe's `collect` (labels and values) records every visible text node of `.brief-chartcard` outside the SVG and outside
`.sr-only`, with its font size, in its `html` field. A fifth button in the periods row with B's visible words (「表示」 and
「すべて」) adds those text nodes to the `html` of every view, so **clause 1 is expected to fail on every view of both probes,
whatever the layers do**. No SVG field is expected to move at 320 / 375 / 390 (the plot's width is the card's, and the row stays
44px high); at 1440 the button takes width from the readout beside the periods, so the card's height may move there (a third
readout row would add 16px).

Order of work, chosen for that reason: the first code commit renders the all-on state exactly as the full package would (the
fifth button in B's words with its accessible name, the layer choice kept for the session, the panel closed and not rendered,
the chart drawn as before). The cut rule is measured on that commit. The panel, the layer flags in `priceChart.js` and the notes
in `chartReadout.js` are built only if all three clauses pass: none of them can change the all-on state's DOM (a closed panel is
not rendered, and the flags all on are today's drawing), so they cannot change clause 1's result.

### What `6398708f` built (reverted by `3d4644e6`; restore with `git revert 3d4644e6`)

- `StockChartCard.jsx`: a fifth button closing the `.brief-periods` row, inside its group 「表示期間」 (the plan's "fifth button in
  the periods row"; §5.6's `.brief-periods button` selector). Visible text 「表示」 over 「すべて」 (`layerWord`: 「すべて」, 「n項目」 or
  「なし」, never a count over a count); accessible name 「チャートに表示するものを選ぶ（いま…）」, which contains no period name: the probe found
  each period button by name on this commit (0 page errors), and by their selectors (read, not run) the timing harness and
  Design Acceptance still find 3か月 / 6か月 / 1年 / /^週足/ alone; `aria-haspopup="dialog"`,
  `aria-expanded="false"`, disabled with the periods when the renderer's chunk fails.
- `chartReadout.js`: `CHART_LAYERS` (水準・収縮・移動平均・出来高・RS線) and `layerWord`, kept there so the component file exports no
  new constant (lint stays at 4 warnings).
- `StockView.jsx`: the hidden layers kept for the tab's session (`useSessionState('brief-chart-layers', [])`), none by default.
- `brief-chart.css`: below 1024px the week button and 「表示」 take their own widths (padding 6px) and the three periods share the
  rest; 「表示」 is two 14px lines at every width (padding 8px at 1024px and wider). A first form with 「表示すべて」 on one line spilt
  the week and 「表示」 buttons at 375 and 390 (the row overflowed by 7 / 4px) and pushed the 1440 readout to a third row (+16px on
  the card: 705.9 → 721.9 on ZZNV); it was not committed (read on the uncommitted working tree with the same scratch geometry
  read, port 4461; those outputs were deleted as superseded).
- Not built: the layer panel (B's sheet), the layer flags in `priceChart.js` and the notes and legend that follow the layers in
  `chartReadout.js`. None of them changes the all-on state's DOM (the panel is closed and not rendered; the flags all on are
  today's drawing), so they could not change clause 1's result, and the cut rule drops the package either way.

### The cut rule, measured on `6398708f` (実測; tree clean, CI-faithful: Chrome for Testing 147 headless shell, the CI fonts, Node 22; port 4461 only, the S1 tree untouched)

| Clause | Measured | Result |
|---|---|---|
| 1. Probe with every layer on byte-identical to the head before | `run-probe.sh <S2 worktree> 4461` (default set: 492 label + 480 value views, 0 page errors) → `probe-6398708.json` 535248ec…; `cmp` against `probe-cd4aaaf.json` 163d66cb… (S2-P3's default probe, the same sha) differs. The harness's `diff.mjs --texts`: **972 of 972 views differ, each in the field `html` only**; every value view's text diff is `+ ["表示","すべて"]`. A scratch classifier over every view and field: removing the two text nodes 「表示」 and 「すべて」 from `html` gives the base's `html` in all 972; no other field differs (no SVG text, label box, candle, line, mask or leader, no chart or SVG box) and no card height moves in any of the 492 label views. | **fails** |
| 2. The periods row at 320 | A scratch Playwright read of every charted fixture stock (30) × each period pressed (the pressed button is bold), 320×640 phone: 5 buttons, smallest 44.0 × 44.0px, one top in all 120 views, no button text spills, the row, the card and the page do not overflow. Also 375 and 390 (smallest 50.0 × 44.0, same checks clean) and 1440 (smallest 50.2 × 32.0; 24px targets there). | passes |
| 3. The card's height (as before, S2-P2b did not run) | The same probe: every one of the 492 label views' `card.h` equals the base's. Distinct values on `6398708f`: 320 492.0 / 508.0; 375 and 390 490.0 / 508.0; 1440 seven values from 643.6 to 727.1 (ZZNV 6か月 705.9, ZZAR 6か月 727.1). | passes |

The oracle probe was not run: one failing file decides clause 1, and the oracle set's chart cards carry the same periods row.

### Why it cannot pass in B's worded form

The probe's `collect` reads every visible text node of `.brief-chartcard` outside the SVG and outside `.sr-only`, so any button in
the periods row whose words are visible adds them to every view. The diff is exactly the stated one: the button's own two
nodes, nothing else. Options for the conductor or the owner (none taken here):

- **Accept the button's words as the probe's one stated difference**, re-wording the cut rule to "byte-identical apart from the
  button's own text nodes"; on these numbers the other two clauses hold in the all-on state, and the rest of the package (panel, flags,
  notes) would then be built on `git revert 3d4644e6`.
- An icon-only button, or its words in `.sr-only` or CSS `content`, would leave `html` unchanged, but it changes B's worded form
  (「表示 すべて」 / 「表示 n項目」, B's own round-2 fix) only to pass a measurement; not built.

### Checks on the head after the revert (実測)

- `git diff cd4aaaf6 HEAD -- frontend` is empty; the frontend tree is `c4e4131e`, S2-P6's, so S2-P6's verified runs (values,
  ledger, surfaces, phrases, heights, the full static suite) carry and were not repeated.
- `run-build.sh` on `3d4644e6` (archive of HEAD, clean): `npm run build` entry 113.87 kB gzip (line 134.00); Design Acceptance
  entry `index-r_-BD7fh.js` 113,559 bytes, as on S2-P6.
- `npx vitest run`: 256 files passed, 1 skipped; 4339 tests passed, 1 skipped.
- `npm run lint`: 0 errors, 4 warnings.
- Hygiene: no frozen file, cap, budget, waiver, token, build target or Design Acceptance tool edited; `positionSizing.js` not
  touched; every commit of the package carries the two trailer lines.
- Tools, checked after the run: the yardstick's tool pin `s1/tools.sha256` (sha 1d06dacd…) 17 of 17 OK, and its harness copies
  9 of 9 OK. The probe's own files (its runner, the two probe specs, the merge and diff scripts) are not in that pin; they are
  dated 11-13, before S1, and the same files reproduced the base `probe-cd4aaaf.json` = S2-P3's `probe-a592864.json` (163d66cb…,
  read from its `.sha256`).

### Not measured here (未検証)

- The button's other words, 「n項目」 and 「なし」, were never rendered (no layer can be hidden on that commit); clause 2 was measured
  with 「すべて」 only.
- The build of `6398708f` itself (its change sits in the stock and readout chunks; it is reverted).
- The oracle probe, the values / ledger / surfaces / phrases runs and the full static suite on `6398708f`.
- The static specs that click every button of the 「表示期間」 group (`brief-chart.spec.js` "no overflow" at 320 / 375 / 414) on
  `6398708f`; the scratch read above covers the same geometry at 320 / 375 / 390.
- Timing (S2-P8 owns it), CI and Design Acceptance.

### Deviations from the order (for the conductor to accept or refuse)

1. The cut rule was measured on the commit that renders the all-on state (the button, the session state, the panel closed),
   not on a full build of the package; the parts not built cannot change clause 1 (above).
2. The oracle base is the head before this package (`probe-cd4aaaf-oracles.json` 57b75ca7…), not S2-P3's file (stale since
   S2-P5's stated ZZPG rename); the oracle probe was not run in the end.
3. The button sits inside the group 「表示期間」 although it is not a period, following the plan's "fifth button in the periods row";
   a later attempt may give it its own group in the same row.

### Owner lines

None reached. Entry 113.87 kB gzip on the head; every card height unchanged on `6398708f`; no cap edited.

### Allow-list delta (this package)

None: `allow/s2.json` stays at 920 entries, sha256 `21731a3aeb80f35f620a65556a9c24b52b73d0a88499a5e51a421a762ac5aa5d`.

### S2-P7 conductor's verdict and open orders

Verdict: **dropped by its cut rule** (clause 1). The conductor reproduced the failure on a quiet machine (load 0.11 at start): the default chart probe on `6398708f` is byte-identical to the worker's run (535248ec…), differs from the base (163d66cb…) in 972 of 972 views, each only by the button's own two visible text nodes 「表示」 and 「すべて」; every other field is equal, 0 page errors. Clauses 2 and 3 pass (320: five buttons on one row, 44.0×44.0 smallest; every card height unchanged, 508 / 492 / 490 on the phone). `brief-chart.spec.js` 53 of 53. The code is reverted at `3d4644e6` (`git diff 6398708f^ 3d4644e6` is empty; the frontend tree equals S2-P6's), so the branch ships what S2-P6 left: entry 113.87 kB gzip (line 134.00), vitest 4339 passed / 1 skipped, lint 0 errors.

Deviations accepted: the cut rule was measured on the all-on commit (the probe reads every visible text node of the chart card, so the unbuilt panel, flags and notes could not have removed the difference); the oracle-probe base is moot (not run); the button sat inside the period group, noted for a re-attempt.

Open question for the owner: B's worded button cannot pass the cut rule as written. Shipping it needs a ruling on three things: rewording the cut rule, plan acceptance item 8 (probe diffs only for S2-P2b and S2-P3), and an allow-list addition for the button's words (plan §5.3 does not list 「表示」, 「すべて」, 「n項目」, 「なし」).

Open orders:
- [minor] The path to shipping above would reword clause 1 to "byte-identical apart from the button's text"; but acceptance item 8 and plan §5.3 gate the words too, so the values yardstick would likely flag them as unlisted additions (a reading: values was never run on `6398708f`).
- [minor] The conductor's round-1 verdict and reproduced numbers were not in the record; they are now.

## S2-P8 Integration, measurement and the record

Status: **done** (record complete; CI and Design Acceptance on the pushed head are the main session's). Started from `37a7b1d5`
(S2-P7's record head, tree clean, frontend tree `c4e4131e`, S2-P6's). Plan S2-P8, §5, §7, §8; the minor orders carried from S2-P3, S2-P5 and S2-P6.

### What is integrated

Exactly the packages that landed: S2-P0 (the S2 baselines), S2-P1 (the call card in B's form), S2-P3 (one name per level, the
chart gutter, the T-number placer fix), S2-P5 (three copy items), S2-P6 (BEH-1, the stop row), and this package's minor orders.
Recorded, not shipped: S2-P2 (the strip; stopped at its owner line O10, reverted at `4920e3af`), S2-P2b and S2-P4 (skipped, since
S2-P2 did not ship), S2-P7 (line toggles; dropped by its cut rule, reverted at `3d4644e6`).

- **Values** (against `s1/` with the cumulative list, and the three S2-P6 states against the S2-P5 head's run of them with their
  own list): the only new differences are in the `names` section, the chart's `img` name with 「、損切りの目安 X」 replaced by

  the conductor accepts or refuses it. No line, SVG text, pair, number, fold or matrix record moves.

the order rotated every run, 390 and 1440 dark, the 11-12 rebuild's data; medians in ms; load before → after each run: 0.29 → 3.37,

|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| S1 against S1 (noise) | 390 | S1 A / S1 B | 2639 / 2626 | 107 / 106 | 268 / 293 | **574 / 611** (spread 37) |
| | 1440 | S1 A / S1 B | 3010 / 2942 | 243 / 272 | 543 / 497 | **513 / 555** (spread 42) |
| S1 first, then S2 | 390 | S1 / S2 | 2577 / 2602 | 143 / 121 | 270 / 269 | **674 / 521** (−153) |
| | 1440 | S1 / S2 | 2857 / 2932 | 270 / 263 | 565 / 580 | **524 / 531** (+7) |
| S2 first, then S1 | 390 | S2 / S1 | 2572 / 2571 | 104 / 102 | 316 / 268 | **626 / 611** (+15) |
| | 1440 | S2 / S1 | 2905 / 2843 | 248 / 293 | 548 / 482 | **519 / 527** (−8) |

**The period switch** (`run-period.sh`, the synthetic fixture on each tree's dev server, CPU 4×, 30 stocks × 4 periods, 120 switches
per width; medians in ms; S1's own port 4462, S2's 4461):

| Run (in order) | Tree | 390 | 1440 | Load before → after |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| base1 (noise) | S1 | 169.0 | 172.5 | 0.44 → 7.36 |
| base2 (noise) | S1 | 169.0 | 172.5 | 0.59 → 2.27 |
| base3 | S1 | 173.5 | 180.0 | 0.52 → 1.62 |
| slice1 | S2 | **163.0** | **176.0** | 0.49 → 1.67 |
| slice2 | S2 | **161.5** | **187.0** | 0.50 → 1.68 |
| base4 | S1 | 178.0 | 187.0 | 0.50 → 2.08 |

**Against acceptance 8 as written ("not above the base medians"):**
- **`stock_open_ms`**: per run, the slice's medians are 521 and 626 at 390 against the base's 674 and 611 in the same runs, and 531
  and 519 at 1440 against 524 and 527 (+7 and −8). The base-against-base spread is 37 (390) and 42 (1440). Three of the four pairs
  lie inside it (+15, +7, −8). The fourth, 390 with S1 first (674 against 521, −153), lies outside it in the slice's favour and is
  weak evidence: its base median of 674 is itself outside the S1-against-S1 medians (574 and 611). Pooled over the same three
  `timing4` runs (7 runs per tree in each; the base tree is in all three): at 390 the base is **611** (n 28) and the slice **579.5**
  (n 14); at 1440 the base is **526.5** (n 28) and the slice **525.5** (n 14). The slice's pooled medians are not above the base's.
- **The period switch**: at 390 both slice medians (163.0, 161.5) are below every base median (169.0 to 178.0). At 1440 slice1
  (176.0) is below the base run before it (180.0) and slice2 (187.0) equals the base run after it (187.0); against the four base
  medians pooled (176.25) slice2 is 10.75 above, inside their spread (172.5 to 187.0, 14.5). The base medians drift upward over the
  session, as S2-P3's runs found (169 → 178 at 390). Run base1 ended at load 7.36 (0.44 → 7.36); base2, the next run (0.59 →
  2.27), repeats its medians exactly (169.0 / 172.5). Not above the base medians beyond the band.
- **Not an acceptance-8 metric, reported as read**: the tab switch is 15 ms above the base in one order at 1440 (580 / 565) and 66
  above in the other (548 / 482), against a base-against-base spread of 46; at 390 −1 and +48 against a spread of 25. The two orders
  disagree in size, and the 1440 slice medians (548, 580) lie inside the four base medians' range (482 to 565) or 15 ms over its top.
  Pooled over the same three `timing4` runs, reported and not judged: at 390 the base is 269.5 (n 28) and the slice 287.5 (n 14); at
  1440 the base is 509.5 and the slice 557 (+47.5, against a base-against-base spread of 46).
  No conclusion is drawn here; the 1440 tab switch is S3's main risk (plan §9.1) and Design Acceptance reads it on CI.

### Deviations from the order (for the conductor to accept or refuse)

1. **Order 1's accessible-name entries are a plan §5.3 deviation**, stated before the run: §5.3's S2 list has no accessible-name
   rename. 48 entries, exact strings, anchored view lists.
2. **My pre-run statement of the probe was wrong** (above): it records the chart's accessible name, so both probes differ from
   S2-P6's, in that field only, by the stated rename.
3. **The screenshot set is defined, not the literal cross product.** Plan §8's S2 sentence (every stock state × 50 / 25 / 0 / unknown
   / late × each period × 320 / 375 / 390 / both PWA sizes / 1440 × light and dark) is about 5,000 PNGs a tree; §5.7 sizes the set at
   about 80 views, and the disk had 4 GB free. Taken: **every stock view of the matrix** (112: 25 stock states, each as published,
   on the 0% day, under the unknown market and on a late publication (the 0% marketHold state as published only); the three
   governing states in 3か月 / 1年 / 週足 as well as 6か月; and ZZNV on the pending and late days at 50%, 25% and 0%) at 320 / 375 / 390 /
   1440, light and dark, **the installed-PWA sizes for the three governing states only** (18 views), and a full-page PNG at 375 and
   1440 for each published stock state. **25% exists only on ZZNV's pending and late views**: the matrix has no 25% market for the
   other stock states. Taken by an unpinned copy of the yardstick's shot tool that differs only in its view filter (`shot` flag
   ignored, stock views only) and its PWA rule; the pinned tools are untouched (17 of 17 OK).
4. **The base tree's screenshots ran beside the S2 values run** (ports 4462 and 4461, different trees, 11:52-12:49 JST), against
   the "one heavy run at a time" habit; screenshots are no byte gate, but the values run then had 2 timeouts (re-run once, passed)
   and the base shots 1 (re-run once, passed). No timing run overlapped anything.
5. **Order 14 took the cheaper option** the order offers: the S2-P5 head's run of the three new states stays their base (copied to
   a durable path, hashes recorded), rather than re-basing them into `s1/` and extending S2-P1..P5's anchors by proof. Their S1 →
   S2-P5 leg therefore stays ungated by the yardstick, as S2-P6 recorded.
6. **The carried `brief-call.spec.js` citation order is not done**: its own rule ties it to the next package that edits that file, and
   no S2 package after S2-P1 did.
7. **The full static suite ran on `56edb80c`**, whose frontend tree equals the final head's (only docs commits follow it).
8. **CONTEXT.md does not define 4つの数字の帯 and 価格の位置**, though plan §7 lists them among the S2 terms: S2-P2 (the strip) was
   reverted at its owner line and S2-P4 (the 価格の位置 list) was skipped, so neither exists in the app. They belong to the package
   that ships them (the reason is in the CONTEXT.md subsection above).
9. **The screenshot set has no stock view under an FTD market** (upgraded, aged or conflict), though plan §8's preamble lists FTD for
   every slice: the yardstick matrix's stock views use the as-published (50%), 0%, unknown and late markets only. The call's market
   line in the FTD states is covered by `surfaces.mjs` (19 of 19 states agree, the run above). Noted for the critics in the head of
   their view index; deviation 3 names only the 25% gap.

### Order 3's mutations (実測; scratch edits of `chartLabels.js`, never committed, the file restored and compared byte for byte)

`npx vitest run src/static/brief/chartLabels.test.js src/static/brief/priceChart.test.js`:

| Mutation | Result |
|---|---|
| none (`f155b8ea`) | 79 passed (chartLabels 25) |
| repair ignores `allow` (the `options.allow` clause dropped from repair's skip line) | **2 failed**: the `allow` test at `chartLabels.test.js:207` (the T number would take the rejected spot; it must stay forced at x = 100) and `priceChart.test.js:1044` (the walled contraction) |
| settle ignores `allow` (`allow: null` in settle's `place` call) | **2 failed**: the `allow` test at `chartLabels.test.js:220` (the settled label with `allow` would take the freed rejected spot) and `priceChart.test.js:1044` |

Before `f155b8ea` the settle mutation could not fail the `allow` test (it never called `settle()`), and the repair mutation failed only
`priceChart.test.js` (S2-P3's verification). The control case (the same settle without `allow`) takes the freed spot, so the test shows
settle runs.

### Deliberate test changes (file:line on `37a7b1d5` / old / new / why; no test was deleted or loosened)

| File:line | Old | New | Why |
|---|---|---|---|
| `priceChart.test.js:122` | `toMatch(/…ピボット 108\.00.*損切りの目安 100\.40.*値幅の収縮 12%→6%/)` | `toMatch(/…ピボット 108\.00.*、損切り 100\.40、.*値幅の収縮 12%→6%/)`, plus a new `not.toContain('損切りの目安')` | Order 1: the accessible name's segment renamed; the new form is pinned with its delimiters and the old name is asserted gone |
| `chartLabels.test.js:203-205` (the `allow` test's last case) | the blocker `other.put(20, along(4, 4, [20]))` (one spot, so it could not move) | the blocker `other.put(20, along(4, 60, [20]))` (a second candle-free spot) and an assert that it stays at x = 4; plus a settle case with its control | Order 3: the test now pins repair and settle (mutation results above); stronger, not looser |
| `brief-stop.spec.js` (new cases only) | — | 4 D8 cases (375 and 1440) and 8 both-themes cases | Orders 15 and 16 |

The mutation table's unmutated row was run as written (`npx vitest run src/static/brief/chartLabels.test.js
src/static/brief/priceChart.test.js` on `f155b8ea`'s files: 79 passed).

### Owner questions of S2 (オーナー向けの選択肢、plan §9.2 の形。数値はこの記録の実測)

スライスS2では次の点を決めていません。どれも出荷した形のまま残し、数字を添えて報告します。

- **O10（4つの数字の帯）**：帯は出していません（S2-P2 が線で止まり、コードは戻しました）。帯の「前提」の行を全文で出すと、停滞の銘柄が公開の遅れた日に 3,138px（線 3,124 を 14px 超過）になりました。帯の余白を0にしても 3,128px、前提の短い形（368px）は2行のままで1行も減りません。前提を「前提」だけの1行（248px）にすれば 14px 減り、計算では 3,124px ちょうどです。線を上げる、前提を外す、帯を出さない、のどれにするかはオーナーの判断です。帯がない間、銘柄画面の値は S2-P1 の仮のタイル（「買いの範囲」「損切り」「株数」）のままです。
- **O3-b（帯の位置）**：帯を作った試し（`a2593468`）では、チャートの上の値札（ピボット・損切り・ゾーン上限）は4つの画面サイズすべてで最初の1画面に入りました。帯の4つの数字は、390×844 の買いゾーンの銘柄だけが入り（787.6px、タブは 788px）、地合い0%の銘柄と決算の近い銘柄は 5.8px 足りません。375×812 では 31.6〜37.8px、ホーム画面に追加した表示では 80.6〜115.8px、タブの下に隠れます。帯を「結論とチャートの間」に置く形（O3-b）はこちらでは選んでいません。
- **O3-a（チャートの数値表示を1行に）**：S2-P2b は帯が出ないため実行していません。空く高さ（約32px）は未検証です。チャートのカードは 508 / 492 / 490px のままです。
- **O11（どの損切りで判定するか）**：出荷したのは「ピボットの −7.0%」で全員同じ判定です。11/12の再計算で 58件が該当、242件が該当なし、315件が判定なしです（615件中）。初期設定では、判定できて計画のある 101件すべてで、計画の損切りと仮の損切りは同じ数字です。損切り幅を5%にした場合と「この位置を使う」を押した場合は、2つの名前で2つの数字を並べます（テストで確認、ZZSD では「仮の損切り $107.14」と「計画の損切り $109.12（構造 −5.3%）」）。計画の損切りそのもので判定する形（a）は作っていません。
- **BEH-2（損切りに到達をサインとして数えるか、と、どの上抜けで判定するか）**：（S2-C1 で行の名前は「仮の損切りに到達（保有なら）」になり、該当のときはタイルの下に「保有なら」の行が出ます。）数えていません（件数・チップ・ウォッチの注意・買いの判定は動きません）。さらに、この行は「最後の上抜け」から判定していますが、「出来高を伴う上抜け後のピボット割れ」は同じ60営業日の中のもっと前の上抜けを指すことがあります。該当の14件のうち3件で日付が違います：CRON（割れの判定 11/4、損切りの行 11/11）、NWL（11/2 と 11/9）、MSFT（11/4 と 11/9）。NWL は割れの判定の日付から見ると 11/6 の安値 5.37 が仮の損切り 5.42 を下回り、該当になります。また「該当なし」の242件のうち57件（ピボット接近16件・買いゾーン超過41件。NWL、RLAY、FROG、STGW、XMTR を含む）は、同じ期間のもっと前の上抜けの後に安値が仮の損切りに届いていました。最後の上抜けで判定する（出荷した形）か、期間の中で最初の日付のある上抜けで判定するかを決めてください。
- **1つの水準に1つの名前（B1の残り）**：チャートは帯の上端を「ゾーン上限 116.28」と呼びますが、S2-P1 の仮のタイルは「買いの範囲」の下にピボットと上端を並べます（「110.74」「〜116.28」）。帯（O10）が出るか、オーナーが O10 を決めるまで、このタイルは名前を変えません。チャートの「ゾーン上限」の札は、11/12の再計算の価格つきの札 194件のうち 94件が上端のそば、41件が52週高値の線に近く、22件がピボットの線に近く（幅の狭いゾーンで札がゾーンの中）、31件が上端から12px以上離れています（S1は191件中 91 / 43 / 21 / 31）。札を動かす場合は、その差分を先に示してから行います。
- **収縮の番号（B7の残り）**：S2-P3 の修正の後、番号が自分の枠ではなく別の収縮の枠の上に来るのは9件です（8件はチャート全体へのフォールバック、1件は RMBI 6か月 320px で中心が T3 の枠に0.1px入る丸めの境目）。事前に決めた B7 の確認は 439件中124件で満たしていません（S1は156件）。これらは自分の枠の横、どの枠の上でもない位置です。フォールバックの修正はこのスライスでは行わず、S3-P4 で再確認します。
- **ピボット接近の距離の書き方**：-0.04% の銘柄では、状態のチップが「ピボット接近 0〜−0.1%」、銘柄画面の結論（銘柄の行）が「ピボット接近（あと0.1%未満）」で、1つの距離に2つの書き方があります（候補・ウォッチの行のチップと、銘柄画面の結論）。結論を「あと0〜」にすると「あと0」と出てしまうため、変えていません。
- **市場タブの「判定していない項目」**：売りのカードは「判定なしの項目 n件」に変えましたが、市場タブのサインの欄はまだ「判定していない項目 n件：…」です（D9 は売りのカードだけを名指ししていました）。変える場合はオーナーの判断で行います。
- **チャートの「表示」ボタン（S2-P7）**：Bの「表示 すべて」のボタンは、条件（全レイヤー表示でチャートの測定が前と完全に同じ）を、ボタン自身の2語「表示」「すべて」のためだけに満たせませんでした。他の条件（320pxで1行・44px、カードの高さ不変）は満たしています。出すには、条件の書き換え、受け入れ条件8、許可リストへのボタンの語の追加の3点の判断が必要です。
- **批評の第1回で増えた質問（S2-C1）**：「銘柄」の行が地合いを言う日、タイルの「試算」の言葉、「買いの範囲」のタイルの名前、スマホの最初の1画面とタイル、「保有なら」の行と BEH-2、折りたたみの要約の「損切り」（最終判定で追加）。数値は S2-C1 の節にあります。
- **S1 から残る質問**：地合いの帯（S1の質問1）はこのスライスでも作っていません。候補・ウォッチのバーには地合いの答えは出ず、銘柄画面は結論カードの地合いの行（S2-P1）で示します。上抜けの名前は定数 `BREAKOUT_HOLD_NAME`（「上抜け維持（確定分）」）のまま、O2-b・更新待ちの見出しの語・鮮度未確認も変えていません。銘柄画面の地合いの行（S2-P1）は、公開が遅れた日や引け後に「地合い 10/2時点の判定・更新待ち（独自判定）・目安 最大50%（10/2時点・独自設定）」の形を使うので、更新待ちの語をオーナーが変える場合はこの行も一緒に変わります。

Code commits: `5453f961`, `738480c9`, `38af1f53`, `5b414e5b`, `c64fbfa5`, `60a21edc`, `2868535e`, `3cc6b089` and `452d12f5` (a spec
only). Record commits: `9b2ee399` (CONTEXT.md and the row's new name), this section, and the one after it that adds the shot set's
count (`a9ef06fe` and the next), then one record-only commit for the conductor's final verdict (its subsection at the end of this
section). The round was cut off by a usage limit after `452d12f5`; the resume re-ran `brief-chart.spec.js`, measured the hit states at
1440, took the extra shot set and the S2 shot set again on `452d12f5`, and wrote this section from the round's notes. Two critics read the integrated slice (the designer's and the trader's, 12 major issues and the minors). This round fixed what plan
and rules allow and records the rest as owner questions with their numbers. Port 4461 only; the S1 tree untouched.

### Readings taken here (stated; the conductor accepts or refuses each)

- **Issues 3 and 9 disagree on the 銘柄 line.** Issue 3 suggests a short form 「銘柄・保有なら損切りに到達」; issue 9 says not to add one,
  since it would push BEH-1 toward being a sign. This round follows issue 9 and the plan (D5's short forms are for sell chips and a
  near earnings date; D7 keeps BEH-1 out of every count and chip): the hit is said once, in the plan's own sentence under the tiles.
- **Issue 5's title is a rename of the plan's row name** (plan S2-P6 wrote 「損切りに到達（保有なら）」). It is taken because D8 names
  the row's level 仮の損切り and the plan's 損切り, and the bare title was the one place where 損切り meant the other level; the
  judgement, its level, the summary segment (「／損切り判定なし」) and the status clause (「損切りの行：…」) are unchanged. CONTEXT.md
  carries the new name with the old one under _Avoid_. **A plan deviation for the conductor to accept or refuse.**
- **The 保有なら line's ink** is the text ink (`--text`), not the note's muted `--text-3`: issue 3 asks for a text-ink line, and D17
  allows any text ink; never `--neg` (D17 keeps it for the word 該当). 12px, as plan S2-P2's strip lines.
- **The line prints the row's money format** (「$105.18」) so it reads the same as the row it points to (the tiles print 105.18; the
  critics' minor on 「$」 is listed below, not changed).
- **Issue 4 stays an owner question**: plan S2-P1 says, for exactly this case, that line 2 is labelled 銘柄 and buyGate's title is
  unchanged on marketHold and marketUnknown days.
- **Issues 8 and 12 were built only as far as a fit probe** (DOM injection on the clean head, no code), since each suggestion was
  conditional on its fit; both failed it (below), so neither shipped.

### Owner questions from this round (オーナー向けの選択肢、plan §9.2 の形。数値はこの節の実測)

- **「銘柄」の行が地合いを言う日（批評 4）**：地合い0%の日と地合い未確認の日、結論カードの2行目は「銘柄」というラベルの下に地合いの見出し（「地合いが新規の買いを控える状態（独自判定）」「地合い未確認（新規の買いは判断を保留）」）を一番大きな文字で出し、その銘柄自身の位置（「位置は参考：買いゾーン・ピボット比 ±0.0%」）は小さい灰色の行になります。1行目の地合いの行と合わせて、地合いを2回言っています。計画（S2-P1）がこの場合も「ラベルは銘柄・見出しはbuyGateのまま」と決めているため、変えていません。この回の値の記録（375幅の銘柄画面112）を見出しの文字で数え直すと、地合い0%の見出しは18画面（地合い0%の日の12状態、ZZNV の地合い保留の状態の4期間、0%の日の発表待ちと公開遅れの2画面）、地合い未確認の見出しは12画面（地合い未確認の日の12状態）です。1440幅も同じ数です。選択肢：(a) 今のまま、(b) その日だけラベルを「結論」などに変える（見出しは変えない・高さ0px）、(c) その日だけ見出しと位置の行の強弱を入れ替える。
- **タイルの「試算」の言葉（批評 8、O10）**：チャートの下の3つのタイルには「試算（買いの推奨ではありません）」の見出しがありません（帯の見出しにする予定でしたが帯は出ていません）。見出しの行を足すと停滞の銘柄が線（3,124px）を超えます（今 3,119px）。高さ0pxで済む方法として、株数のタイルの小さなラベルを「試算・既定条件」、0%の日は「参考・地合い 0%」にする形を測りましたが、幅320pxで入りません：「試算・既定条件」は98.0pxでタイル（90.7px）を15.3pxはみ出し、「参考・地合い 0%」は106.7pxで320px・375px・390pxのどれでも入りません（はみ出し24.0 / 5.7 / 0.7px）。「参考・地合い未確認」は122.0px（39.3 / 21.0 / 16.0px）。「試算」だけなら38.0pxで入ります（320pxで残り44.7px）。どの言葉をどこに出すかは O10 と一緒に決めていただく必要があります。
- **「買いの範囲」のタイルの名前（批評 12、1つの水準に1つの名前）**：タイルを「ピボット 110.74」、下の小さな行を「ゾーン上限 116.28」にする形を測りました。小さな行が320・375・390pxのすべてで2行になり、375pxでタイルが18px高くなって、停滞の銘柄が公開の遅れた日に 3,137px（線 3,124 を13px超過）になります。このため名前は変えていません（帯の O10 と一緒の判断です）。
- **スマホの最初の1画面とタイル（批評 2、O10・O3-b と一緒に）**：スマホではタイルとチップがチャートの下にあり、375×812ではタイルの上端（755.6px）がタブの上端（756px）とほぼ同じ高さのため、タイルの小さなラベル（既定条件・収縮の中・参考）の上半分だけがタブの上に見えます（S2-P1 の測定）。1440幅はこの回でタイルとチップをチャートの上に戻しました（チャートの出来高の見出しは 847.4px、公開が遅れた日 881.4px。ただし公開が遅れた日の3画面（ピボット未確認、損切りの行が該当の2つ）は 909.8px で、900px を9.8px超えます）。スマホで直すには、(a) 帯（O10）を出す、(b) 帯かタイルをチャートの上に置く（O3-b）、(c) チャートの下に1行の要約（「損切り 102.99・80株・リスク $625」）を足す、のいずれかが必要で、(c) は停滞の銘柄で線を超える見込みです（1行で約+28px・未検証）。
- **「保有なら」の行と BEH-2**：損切りの行が「該当」のとき、タイルの下に「保有なら：仮の損切り $105.18 に到達しています（売りのカード）」と出します。株数の試算は残し（D10）、売りのサインの件数・チップ・買いの判定には数えません（BEH-2 は未決のまま）。375pxではこの行は2行（約49px）で、該当の銘柄画面は 2,652 → 2,701px（ZZAR、売りのサインなし）、2,852 → 2,901px（ZZAR、ブレイク失敗のサインあり）になり、線の内側です。1440幅では1行で、チャートが28.4px下がります。公開が遅れた日には出来高の見出しの下端が 909.8px になり、画面の高さ900pxを9.8px超えます（1440幅に高さの線はありません）。11/12の再計算では「該当」は58件で、そのうち55件がピボットの下に戻った銘柄です。銘柄の行の短い形（「銘柄・保有なら損切りに到達」）は足していません（サインのように読めるため）。数えるかどうかは BEH-2 の判断です。
- **折りたたみの要約の「損切り」（最終判定、1つの水準に1つの名前）**：スマホで損切りの行が静かなとき（該当なし・判定なし）、売りのカードの折りたたみの要約は2つの件数の後に「／損切り判定なし」か「／損切り該当なし」を付けます。状態の文の「損切りの行」と同じく、この「損切り」は行の名前で、判定に使う水準は仮の損切り（ピボットの −7.0%）です。価格の横に出る「損切り」は、いつも計画の損切りです。この回の値の記録（全画面）では16画面が「／損切り該当なし」で、そのうち4画面（ZZSD、構造の損切り）は計画の損切りが別の値の 109.12 で、上抜けの後の最安値 109.67 はそれを割っていません。計画の損切りを割っているのに「該当なし」と出る画面はありません（起こりうるのは、ピボットを基準にした銘柄で損切り幅を7.0%より狭く設定したときです）。要約でも水準を名乗り「／仮の損切り該当なし」にしますか。その場合は375幅の要約の行（285px）に入るかを先に測る必要があります：S2-P6 では「／損切りの行 判定なし」が311.3pxで2行になりました。「／仮の損切り該当なし」の幅は未検証です。選択肢：(a) 今のまま、(b) 要約を「／仮の損切り該当なし」「／仮の損切り判定なし」にする（375幅で1行に入る場合）。（数値は最終判定と S2-P6 の実測）

### Deviations from the order (for the conductor to accept or refuse)

1. The stop row's title is renamed (plan S2-P6's 「損切りに到達（保有なら）」 → 「仮の損切りに到達（保有なら）」); see the readings.
2. Four `S2-P5` entries that already matched nothing on the S2-P8 head (「サインなし 4件・判定なし 4件」, 「サインなし 3件・判定なし 4件」,
   closed and opened; S2-P6's summary segment superseded them) were removed as well: a dead entry would let the summary without its stop
   segment come back.
3. The 保有なら line got one follow-up commit (`3cc6b089`) after its first form was seen in this round's own extra shot (「（売りのカー」 /
   「ド）」 at 375): the level and the card name are each one unbreakable unit; same text.
4. CONTEXT.md was committed after the chain had started its first stage (the unit suite), so the chain was stopped and restarted
   from the start on that head (`9b2ee399`); no frontend file differs between `3cc6b089` and `9b2ee399`.
5. **The tiles and chips above the chart at 1024px and wider** (`738480c9`, issues 2 and 7): S2-P1 / D5's order (the level tiles and
   the chips under the chart card) is kept on the phone only; at 1024px and wider `StockVerdict` puts them between the conclusion card
   and the chart, as on the S1 head, until S3-P4 sizes the pane. Accepted in the conductor's final verdict.
6. **The buy-zone reason** (`c64fbfa5`, issue 10): after a dated breakout before the analysis day, a buy-zone row's 判定なし reason is
   「買いゾーンの銘柄は判定しません・計画の買値は M/D 終値」 in place of plan S2-P6's sentence (「計画の買値は M/D 終値・その後の日足はまだありません」),
   words only (state, cause and counts unchanged), on 110 of 114 zone rows of the 11-12 rebuild (実測, the stop mirror rows above);
   the other 4 (a breakout on the analysis day or none dated) keep the plan's sentence. Accepted in the conductor's final verdict.
7. **The two level clauses** (`5b414e5b`, issue 5): the stop row's level says 「・計画の損切りと同じ」 when the plan's stop prints the
   same, and its detail ends 「・判定は仮の損切りで行います」 when the two differ (words added to plan S2-P6's row). Accepted in the
   conductor's final verdict.
8. **The 1440 volume caption past the first screen on three late views** (the conductor's 実測, "The 1440 stock pane" above): 3 of 112
   stock views end the caption at 909.8 (top 895.8), `stock-nopivot@late` (S1 860.2), `stock-stopalone@late` and `stock-stopsign@late`
   (S1 889.9); the S1 tree has 0 of 112 over 900 (max 889.9); `stock-touch@pub` 847.4, `@late` 881.4. Not an owner line (1440 has no
   height line); not changed here: the conductor's final verdict carries it to S3-P4 (item 5 of "Orders carried to a later
   package").

### S2-C1 conductor's final verdict (verdict pass) and its five minor orders

Verdict: **pass** on `75f1f609` (frontend tree `0ef15aae`, unchanged since `452d12f5`), entry 113.91 kB. The five minor orders are
record only and are carried out in one commit that changes this record and CONTEXT.md and no frontend file
(`git rev-parse HEAD:frontend` is `0ef15aae…` before and after), so the verdict carries to it. Nothing was re-run for this commit:
the numbers in this subsection are the conductor's 実測 on `75f1f609` and the S1 head `9402b05d` (CI-faithful: Chrome for Testing 147
headless shell, the CI fonts, Node 22; port 4461) or CI runs it read, except S2-P8's balanced run, which is S2-P8's own (its
"Timing").

The verdict accepted this round's readings and deviations 1 to 7 (deviation 8 it carries to S3-P4): the row's title 「仮の損切りに到達（保有なら）」, the two level clauses,
the buy-zone reason (words only, on 110 of 114 zone rows), the tiles and chips above the chart at 1024px and wider (until S3-P4 sizes
the pane), the 保有なら line in `--text` at 12px with the row's `$` money format, issue 9's reading over issue 3's (no 銘柄 short form,
BEH-2 untouched), the four dead S2-P5 entries removed, the follow-up commit `3cc6b089` and the chain restart on `9b2ee399`.

- **Deviations and the 1440 caption (order 1)**: deviations 5 to 8 are added; the last paragraph under "The 1440 stock pane" gives the
  conductor's probe of all 112 stock views (three late views end the caption at 909.8; the S1 tree has none over 900); the owner
  bullet 「スマホの最初の1画面とタイル」 gives 847.4 / 881.4 and the three 909.8 views in place of the fit probe's 850 / 884.
- **CI, Design Acceptance and timing (order 2)**: below, with the owner's report.
- **The bare 損切り (order 3)**: CONTEXT.md's 仮の損切りに到達（保有なら） now says a bare 損切り beside a price on the stock screen is
  always the plan's stop, and that the fold summary's 「／損切り判定なし」「／損切り該当なし」 and the status clause's 「損切りの行」 name
  this row; the fold summary's owner question is added to "Owner questions from this round"; the `STOP_ROW_LABEL` comment is item 4 of
  "Orders carried to a later package".
- **The 1440 caption in every stock state (order 4)**: item 5 of "Orders carried to a later package" (S3-P4).
- **The 1440 longest initial task (order 5)**: item 6 (S3-P0).

**CI on `75f1f609`** (the conductor's 実測, read through the GitHub API; PR #149, draft):
- CI run 46010330957: every check green (Frontend with lint at 0 errors, Static Browser Regression, Backend, Compose); Static Browser
  Regression **922 passed**.
- Design Acceptance run 46010331082: **86/86**, 0 failures, 28 captures with 0 findings, and **1 deferred timing warning**: the 1440
  `longest_initial_task_ms` median **246** > 200 (runs 177 / 246 / 248; first candidate 2311 / 2819 / 2619).
- The same frontend tree on `452d12f5` read 158 (168 / 158 / 155) with no warning; `f61e934d` (the S2-P8 frontend) read 238.

**Timing** (the conductor's 実測, 19:03-19:19Z; `timing4`, CPU 4x, 7 runs per tree, 390 and 1440 dark, behind `wait-quiet.sh 0.5 600`;
the S1 head `9402b05d` against `75f1f609`; load at the start 0.49 / 0.45 / 0.44, a `ps` before and after showing only the run's own
browser; medians in ms):
- Noise, S1 against S1: stock open 534 / 577 at 390 and 593 / 540 at 1440 (the 1440 longest initial task 281 / 279).
- Both orders: stock open 585 / 594 and 585 / 532 at 390; 575 / 553 and 528 / 513 at 1440.
- Pooled (base n 28, slice n 14): stock open 568.5 / 589.5 at 390 (+21, inside the 43 band) and 554 / 537.5 at 1440: opening a stock
  is not slower than on the S1 head beyond the noise band (acceptance 8).
- The 1440 longest initial task reads +32 (264 → 296) and +35 (235 → 270) in both orders; pooled 269 / 279.5 (+10.5), against a base
  whose run medians span 235-281. Not an acceptance-8 metric, and not judged a regression. S2-P8's balanced run of the pre-S2-C1 tree
  went the other way (270 / 263, 293 / 248). The isolating pair (the S2-P8 build `3c33d4f8` against `75f1f609`) overlapped a foreign
  test suite, was invalid and was stopped, so S2-C1's own effect on this number is 未検証. Carried to S3-P0 (item 6).
- The period switch (`run-period.sh`) was not re-run on this tree (未検証; S2-P8's numbers cover its own tree only).

**オーナーへの報告（Design Acceptance の時間の警告、plan §5.7。数値は最終判定の実測）**：PR の先頭 `75f1f609` の Design Acceptance（実行 46010331082）は86件すべて合格で、時間の警告が1件あります。1440幅の最初の読み込みで一番長いタスク（longest_initial_task_ms）の中央値が 246ms で、予算の 200ms を超えました（3回は 177 / 246 / 248ms）。同じフロントエンドを `452d12f5` で測ったときは 158ms（168 / 158 / 155ms）で警告はなく、S2-P8 の `f61e934d` では 238ms でした。最終判定の計測（CPU を4倍遅くした条件、各7回、2つの順番）では、この値は S1 の先頭より S2 の先頭が +32ms（264 → 296ms）と +35ms（235 → 270ms）長く、まとめると 269 / 279.5ms（+10.5ms）です。S1 の先頭の各回の中央値には 235〜281ms の幅があります。この値は S2 の受け入れ条件8の指標ではなく、悪化とは判定していません。S3-P0 で、S3 が3つのペインを加える前に、S1 の先頭と S2-P8 のフロントエンドのそれぞれと S2 の先頭を比べて測り直します。受け入れ条件8の指標の銘柄画面を開く時間は、S1 の先頭より雑音の幅を超えて遅くはなっていません（390幅 568.5 / 589.5ms、+21ms は雑音の幅 43ms の中）。もう1つの指標の表示期間の切り替えの時間は、このフロントエンドでは測り直していません。
